        Idea:
Evolve the new ArtAgents node MVP toward code-first folder nodes with the smallest practical change. Replace or supplement JSON-only curated external nodes with folder-based nodes where node.py is source of truth, plus optional SKILL.md and requirements.txt/pyproject.toml. Add a minimal @node/NodeSpec authoring API in code, discover folders containing node.py without importing untrusted node code in the main process if practical, and convert the Moirae curated example into a real folder node. Add requirements handling using uv at explicit pipeline.py nodes install <node_id> time, with built-ins using host env and external nodes defaulting to isolated per-node envs. Keep schema/data simpler, preserve legacy pipeline behavior, and do not build marketplace/graph machinery. Add focused tests for folder discovery, code-first spec extraction, install command construction or dry-run behavior, and Moirae folder node metadata. Avoid overwriting unrelated dirty worktree changes.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents

        Idea:
Evolve the new ArtAgents node MVP toward code-first folder nodes with the smallest practical change. Replace or supplement JSON-only curated external nodes with folder-based nodes where node.py is source of truth, plus optional SKILL.md and requirements.txt/pyproject.toml. Add a minimal @node/NodeSpec authoring API in code, discover folders containing node.py without importing untrusted node code in the main process if practical, and convert the Moirae curated example into a real folder node. Add requirements handling using uv at explicit pipeline.py nodes install <node_id> time, with built-ins using host env and external nodes defaulting to isolated per-node envs. Keep schema/data simpler, preserve legacy pipeline behavior, and do not build marketplace/graph machinery. Add focused tests for folder discovery, code-first spec extraction, install command construction or dry-run behavior, and Moirae folder node metadata. Avoid overwriting unrelated dirty worktree changes.

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: artagents/nodes/api.py\n@@\n-        raw = {\n+        raw = {\n             \"id\": id,\n             \"name\": name,\n             \"kind\": kind,\n             \"version\": version,\n-            \"description\": description,\n             \"inputs\": [_to_plain(input_) for input_ in inputs],\n             \"outputs\": [_to_plain(output) for output in outputs],\n             \"conditions\": [_to_plain(condition) for condition in conditions],\n             \"metadata\": dict(metadata or {}),\n         }\n+        if description:\n+            raw[\"description\"] = description\n@@\n def _to_plain(value: Any) -> Any:\n     if isinstance(value, NodeSpec):\n         return value.to_dict()\n     if isinstance(value, NodeDefinition):\n         return value.to_dict()\n     if is_dataclass(value):\n-        return asdict(value)\n+        return _drop_blank_defaults(asdict(value))\n     return value\n+\n+\n+def _drop_blank_defaults(value: Any) -> Any:\n+    if isinstance(value, dict):\n+        return {\n+            [REDACTED](item)\n+            for key, item in value.items()\n+            if item is not None and item != \"\"\n+        }\n+    if isinstance(value, list):\n+        return [_drop_blank_defaults(item) for item in value]\n+    if isinstance(value, tuple):\n+        return tuple(_drop_blank_defaults(item) for item in value)\n+    return value\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
Evolve the new ArtAgents node MVP toward code-first folder nodes with the smallest practical change. Replace or supplement JSON-only curated external nodes with folder-based nodes where node.py is source of truth, plus optional SKILL.md and requirements.txt/pyproject.toml. Add focused tests for folder discovery, code-first spec extraction, install command construction or dry-run behavior, and Moirae folder node metadata. The MVP manifest does not install Moirae or its external binaries.\",\n+    inputs=[\n+        NodePort(\n+            \"screenplay\",\n+            \"file\",\n+            required=True,\n+            description=\"Screenplay file consumed by Moirae.\",\n+        )\n+    ],\n+    outputs=[\n+        NodeOutput(\n+            \"video\",\n+            \"file\",\n+            mode=\"create_or_replace\",\n+            placeholder=\"output\",\n+            description=\"Rendered video path produced by Moirae.\",\n+        )\n+    ],\n+    command={\n+        \"argv\": [\n+            \"{python_exec}\",\n+            \"-m\",\n+            \"moirae\",\n+            \"{screenplay}\",\n+            \"-o\",\n+            \"{output}\",\n+        ]\n+    },\n+    cache={\"mode\": \"none\"},\n+    conditions=[{\"kind\": \"requires_input\", \"input\": \"screenplay\"}],\n+    graph={\n+        \"consumes\": [\"screenplay\"],\n+        \"provides\": [\"video\"],\n+    },\n+    isolation={\n+        \"mode\": \"subprocess\",\n+        \"requirements\": [\"moirae\"],\n+        \"binaries\": [\"asciinema\", \"agg\", \"ffmpeg\"],\n+        \"network\": False,\n+    },\n+    metadata={\n+        \"homepage\": \"https://github.com/peteromallet/Moirae\",\n+        \"manifest_only\": True,\n+        \"binary_requirements_enforced\": \"explicit_check_only\",\n+    },\n+)\n*** Add File: artagents/nodes/curated/moirae/requirements.txt\n+moirae\n*** Add File: artagents/nodes/curated/moirae/SKILL.md\n+# Moirae\n+\n+Curated ArtAgents node metadata for invoking Moirae against a screenplay file.\n+\n+This folder is metadata-only for the node MVP.       "question": "Does the new authoring API normalize to the existing `NodeDefinition` validation path and avoid a parallel schema model?",

      "verdict": ""
    },

      "id": "SC3",
      "task_id": "T3",
      "question": "Can a folder containing `node.py` be discovered and validated deterministically without importing that node code into the main process?",
      "executor_note": "Yes. `discover_folder_node_roots()` finds folders containing `node.py`, and `load_folder_node()` executes `node.py` only in a separate Python subprocess using `runpy.run_path()`.       "depends_on": [

      "status": "done",
      "executor_notes": "Updated external runner command expansion so `{python_exec}` no longer defaults to the host interpreter for external subprocess nodes. `NodeRunRequest.python_exec` now defaults to `None`, making explicit request overrides distinguishable; `python_exec` input overrides still win; explicit `NodeRunRequest.python_exec` overrides win next; otherwise external subprocess nodes use `node_python_path(node)` from the install helper only when the installed env Python exists. If an external subprocess node requires `{python_exec}` and no env or override exists, `NodeRunnerError` names `python3 pipeline.py nodes install <node_id>` and does not auto-install.         Idea:
Evolve the new ArtAgents node MVP toward code-first folder nodes with the smallest practical change. Replace or supplement JSON-only curated external nodes with folder-based nodes where node.py is source of truth, plus optional SKILL.md and requirements.txt/pyproject.toml. Add a minimal @node/NodeSpec authoring API in code, discover folders containing node.py without importing untrusted node code in the main process if practical, and convert the Moirae curated example into a real folder node. Add requirements handling using uv at explicit pipeline.py nodes install <node_id> time, with built-ins using host env and external nodes defaulting to isolated per-node envs. Keep schema/data simpler, preserve legacy pipeline behavior, and do not build marketplace/graph machinery. Add focused tests for folder discovery, code-first spec extraction, install command construction or dry-run behavior, and Moirae folder node metadata. Avoid overwriting unrelated dirty worktree changes.

NODE TEST - DAY', encoding='utf-8')\n    base = ['python3', 'pipeline.py', 'nodes', 'run', 'external.moirae', '--out', str(Path(tmp) / 'out'), '--input', f'screenplay={screenplay}', '--dry-run']\n\n    missing = subprocess.run(base, cwd=repo, text=True, capture_output=True, check=False)\n    print('missing_rc', missing.returncode)\n    print('missing_stderr_has_install', 'pipeline.py nodes install external.moirae' in missing.stderr)\n\n    override = subprocess.run(base[:-1] + ['--input', 'python_exec=/tmp/explicit-python', '--dry-run'], cwd=repo, text=True, capture_output=True, check=False)\n    print('override_rc', override.returncode)\n    print('override_stdout_starts_override', override.stdout.startswith('/tmp/explicit-python -m moirae'))\n\n    if not python_path.exists():\n        python_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        python_path.write_text('# fake python\\n', encoding='utf-8')\n        (node_env_root / '.codex-created').write_text('1\\n', encoding='utf-8')\n        created_env = True\n    installed = subprocess.run(base, cwd=repo, text=True, capture_output=True, check=False)\n    print('installed_rc', installed.returncode)\n    print('installed_stdout_starts_env_python', installed.stdout.startswith(str(python_path)))\n\nif created_env and (node_env_root / '.codex-created').exists():\n    shutil.rmtree(node_env_root)\nPY",

      "executor_notes": "Updated external runner command expansion so `{python_exec}` no longer defaults to the host interpreter for external subprocess nodes. `NodeRunRequest.python_exec` now defaults to `None`, making explicit request overrides distinguishable; `python_exec` input overrides still win; explicit `NodeRunRequest.python_exec` overrides win next; otherwise external subprocess nodes use `node_python_path(node)` from the install helper only when the installed env Python exists.         "python3 - <<'PY'\nimport shutil\nimport subprocess\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom artagents.nodes.install import node_python_path\nfrom artagents.nodes.registry import load_default_registry\n\nrepo = Path.cwd()\nnode = load_default_registry().get('external.moirae')\npython_path = node_python_path(node)\nnode_env_root = python_path.parents[2]\ncreated_env = False\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    screenplay = Path(tmp) / 'screenplay.md'\n    screenplay.write_text('EXT. NODE TEST - DAY', encoding='utf-8')\n    base = ['python3', 'pipeline.py', 'nodes', 'run', 'external.moirae', '--out', str(Path(tmp) / 'out'), '--input', f'screenplay={screenplay}', '--dry-run']\n\n    missing = subprocess.run(base, cwd=repo, text=True, capture_output=True, check=False)\n    print('missing_rc', missing.returncode)\n    print('missing_stderr_has_install', 'pipeline.py nodes install external.moirae' in missing.stderr)\n\n    override = subprocess.run(base[:-1] + ['--input', 'python_exec=/tmp/explicit-python', '--dry-run'], cwd=repo, text=True, capture_output=True, check=False)\n    print('override_rc', override.returncode)\n    print('override_stdout_starts_override', override.stdout.startswith('/tmp/explicit-python -m moirae'))\n\n    if not python_path.exists():\n        python_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        python_path.write_text('# fake python\\n', encoding='utf-8')\n        (node_env_root / '.codex-created').write_text('1\\n', encoding='utf-8')\n        created_env = True\n    installed = subprocess.run(base, cwd=repo, text=True, capture_output=True, check=False)\n    print('installed_rc', installed.returncode)\n    print('installed_stdout_starts_env_python', installed.stdout.startswith(str(python_path)))\n\nif created_env and (node_env_root / '.codex-created').exists():\n    shutil.rmtree(node_env_root)\nPY",
